# Hero power database

Auto-generated by `HdtArenaHelper.Training -- --dump-database` from **HearthDb 36.2.0.0**
(the card DB bundled with HDT, pinned by `hdt-version.txt` — not by
`global.json`, which pins the SDK), grouped by `CardSet` — do not hand-edit, it is
overwritten by `.github/workflows/card-database.yml`.

Cards, hero powers and heroes are three separate files here and three separate
databases in `Generated/HSDatabase.g.cs`, which the test projects compile; a drift
test fails if any of them stops matching HearthDb.
